Function softmax_x(x, center=True):
  x = np.asarray(x,float)
  if center: x = x - x.mean()
  # clip to prevent overflow? soft: x = np.clip(x, -50, 50)
  x = x - x.max()
  ax = np.exp(x)
  return ax/ax.sum()

Objective/Jac:

Define:
```
OBJ_TAU = 50.0

def softmax_from_x(x): ...
def obj_grad(x):
    a = softmax_from_x(x)
    c = fast_conv(a)
    # c sum=1
    # logsumexp stable
    cm = c.max()
    exp = np.exp(OBJ_TAU*(c-cm))
    p = exp/exp.sum()
    val = float(cm*OBJ_TAU + np.log(exp.sum())/OBJ_TAU)  # logsumexp
    # grad c wrt a
    tmp = fast_cross_conv(p, a[::-1])
    g_a = 2.0*tmp[len(a)-1:2*len(a)-1]
    # ensure length n
    # softmax grad: g_x = a*(g_a - sum(a*g_a))
    shift = float(np.dot(a,g_a))
    g_x = a*(g_a - shift)
    # optional project: g_x -= g_x.mean()
    return val, g_x
```
Need check `fast_cross_conv(p, a[::-1])` expects lengths m,n, returns m+n-1. m=2n-1, nrev=n => length 3n-2. slice [n-1:2n-1] length n. Good.
`val` maybe not include constant log 2n; okay. L-BFGS minimizes logM approx.

Adam in log-softmax:
```
def adam_logsoftmax(a0, deadline, tau=50.0, lr=0.01, max_check=...):
  global OBJ_TAU
  OBJ_TAU=tau
  x=np.log(a0+eps)-np.log(a0+eps).mean()
  m=np.zeros(n); v=np.zeros(n); t=0
  best=a0; best_J=compute_J(a0)
  last_improve=0
  for t in range(max_iters):
    val, g = obj_grad(x)  # scaled by n
    # maybe normalize g?
    m=0.9*m+0.1*g
    v=0.999*v+0.001*g*g
    mhat=m/(1-0.9**t); vhat=v/(1-0.999**t)
    step=lr*mhat/(np.sqrt(vhat)+1e-8)
    x = x - step
    x = x - x.mean()
    a=softmax_from_x(x)
    J=compute_J(a)
    if J<best_J*... update
    if no improve 500: maybe increase tau? perturb
```

LP solve:
```
def solve_lp(a, M, top_frac=1.0, time_limit=5, use_full=False):
 n=len(a)
 c=fast_conv(a)
 if top_frac>=1: active=np.arange(2*n-1)
 else:
   thresh=np.percentile(c, (1-top_frac)*100)
   active=np.where(c>=thresh)[0]
   if len(active)<max(50,0.1*n): active=np.argsort(c)[-int(0.5*n):]
 A=build_conv_matrix(a)
 if len(active)<2*n-1: A_sub=A[active,:]
 else A_sub=A
 c_obj=-np.ones(n)
 b_ub=np.full(len(active), M)
 bounds=[(0,None)]*n # but list of tuples no lambda
 r=linprog(c_obj, A_ub=A_sub, b_ub=b_ub, bounds=bounds, method='highs', options={'time_limit': time_limit, 'presolve': True})
 if r.success:
   g=np.maximum(r.x,0)
   # verify? compute cross conv a,g; if max <= M*1.001 use; else for reduced fallback full.
```

Let's design propose_candidate time flow precisely:
```
def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1500, **kwargs):
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    deadline = time.monotonic() + float(budget_s) - 30.0
    best = BestTracker()
    # parent
    try: parent = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, float); parent=np.maximum(parent,0); if finite...
    except: parent=None
    if parent is not None:
        best.update(parent, 'parent')
    else: parent = np.ones(256); best.update(parent,'fallback')
    # Define lengths and start counts
    if budget_s > 1200: lengths=[1024, 2048, 512]
    else: lengths=[1024, 512]
    for n in lengths:
       if time.monotonic() >= deadline - 200: break
       # if n too large and time left <600 maybe skip 2048
       starts = generate_start_list(n, parent, rng, limit=...)
       # evaluate and sort
       scored=[]
```

`run_lbfgs`:
```
def run_lbfgs(x0, tau, maxfun, maxiter):
    global OBJ_TAU
    OBJ_TAU = float(tau)
    x0 = np.asarray(x0, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(x0)):
        x0 = np.zeros_like(x0)
    # center
    x0 = x0 - x0.mean()
    try:
       res = minimize(obj_grad, x0, method='L-BFGS-B', jac=True, options={'maxiter': int(maxiter), 'maxfun': int(maxfun), 'ftol': 1e-10, 'gtol': 1e-7, 'maxls': 30, 'disp': False})
       if res.x is not None and np.all(np.isfinite(res.x)):
           return res.x - res.x.mean()
    except Exception as e:
       print('lbfgs error', e)
    return x0
```

```
def compute_J(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,float)
    a=np.maximum(a,0)
    s=a.sum()
    if s < 0.01: return inf
```

Let's implement `solve_lp` carefully for time.

```
def build_conv_matrix(a):
    n = len(a)
    first_col = np.zeros(2*n-1)
    first_col[:n] = a
    first_row = np.zeros(n)
    first_row[0] = a[0]
    A = sparse_toeplitz(first_col, first_row, format='csr')
    return A

def solve_lp(a, M, top_frac, time_limit):
    n=len(a)
    if M <= 0: return None
    c = fast_conv(a)
    L = 2*n-1
    if top_frac >= 1.0 - 1e-9:
        active = np.arange(L)
    else:
        k = max(20, int(L*top_frac))
        # use percentiles maybe faster: threshold=np.partition(c, L-k)[L-k]; active=np.where(c>=threshold)
        part_val = np.partition(c, max(0, L-k))[max(0,L-k)]
        active = np.where(c >= part_val)[0]
        if active.size < k:
            active = np.argsort(c)[-k:]
        # include all exactly near max? okay.
    A = build_conv_matrix(a)
    if active.size < L:
        A_sub = A.tocsr()[active, :]
    else:
        A_sub = A.tocsr()
    A_sub = A_sub * (1.0/M)
    c_obj = -np.ones(n)
    b_ub = np.ones(active.size)
    try:
       res = linprog(c_obj, A_ub=A_sub, b_ub=b_ub, bounds=(0,None), method='highs', options={'time_limit': time_limit, 'presolve': True})
       if res.success:
          g=np.maximum(res.x,0)
          # verify
          ag=fast_cross_conv(a,g)
          if ag.size and ag.max() <= M*(1+1e-4)+1e-15:
             return g
          # If reduced violated, try full only if n not too large and not already full
          if active.size < L and n <= 1024:
             A_full = A.tocsr() * (1.0/M)
             res2=linprog(c_obj,A_full,np.ones(L),bounds=(0,None),method='highs',options={'time_limit':time_limit,'presolve':True})
             if res2.success:
                 g2=np.maximum(res2.x,0)
                 if fast_cross_conv(a,g2).max() <= M*(1+1e-4)+1e-15:
                    return g2
             return g
          return g
    except Exception:
       pass
    return None
```
Potential memory: For n=2048 full A 4M nonzeros, building then subset. `A_sub` row subset copies. okay.

Time limit for linprog: If time_limit too low, may return not success. We can try without presolve? HiGHS presolve helpful. Use `time_limit=2.0` for full n≤768, `1.0` otherwise. Let's implement a simple `fast_gradient_optimize` that uses our `obj_grad` but on x with Adam, no SciPy minimize. It can run for limited iterations. This can also screen starts cheaper.

`adam_optimize(x0, deadline, tracker, label, tau, max_iters)`:
```
def adam_optimize_x(x0, tau, max_iters, n_seed, tracker, prefix):
    global OBJ_TAU
    OBJ_TAU=tau
    x=np.array(x0,copy); x-=x.mean()
    m=np.zeros_like(x); v=np.zeros_like(x); t=0; lr=0.01? 
    beta1=0.9; beta2=0.999; eps=1e-8
    best_x=x; best_J=inf
    for t in range(max_iters):
       val,g=obj_grad(x)
       if not finite break
       t+=1
       m=beta1*m+(1-beta1)*g
       v=beta2*v+(1-beta2)*g*g
       mhat=m/(1-beta1**t); vhat=v/(1-beta2**t)
       x = x - lr * mhat/(np.sqrt(vhat)+eps)
       x = x - x.mean()
       # every 50 eval compute J
       a=softmax_from_x(x); J=compute_J(a)
       if J<best_J: best_x=x.copy(); best_J=J; tracker.update_score(a,J,prefix)
    return best_x
```

Let's implement `softmax_from_x` stable:
```
def softmax_from_x(x):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64)
    if x.size == 0: return x
    x = x - x.mean()  # optional scale invariant, keeps range smaller? Mean not max; if range huge, still. Then x = x - x.max()
    x_max = x.max()
    ex = np.exp(x - x_max)
    s = ex.sum()
    if s <=0 or not finite: ex = np.ones_like(x); s = x.size
    a = ex/s
    return a
```
If x has extremely large values, x.mean maybe overflow? x from x0 finite and L-BFGS maybe finite. Use `np.nan_to_num`? Could do if not finite reset. In obj_grad, if not finite return inf.

```
def make_x_from_a(a):
    a=np.maximum(a,0.0); s=a.sum(); if s<1e-300: return zeros
    a=a/s
    a=np.clip(a,1e-50, None)
    x=np.log(a)
    x-=x.mean()
    return x
```

```
def generate_start_list(n, parent, rng, limit=20):
    starts=[]
    t=np.linspace(0,1,n,endpoint=False)
    # parent
    if parent is not None:
       p=resample_seq(parent,n)
       if p.sum()>0: starts.append(('parent', p))
       p2=np.maximum(p + 0.05*p.mean()*rng.normal(0,1,n), 0.01*p.mean()); starts.append(('parent_noise', p2))
    # power
    idx=np.arange(1,n+1,dtype=float)
    for alpha in [0.25,0.35,0.45,0.5,0.55,0.65]:
       a=idx**(-alpha); starts.append((f'pow{alpha}', a)) # no f-string? okay, understood text.
```

```
def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1500, **kwargs):
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    start_time=time.monotonic()
    deadline=start_time+float(budget_s)-30
    tracker=BestTracker()
    parent=load_parent()
    if parent is not None: tracker.update(parent,'parent')
    else: tracker.update(np.ones(256),'ones')
    lengths=[]
    if time.monotonic() < deadline-400: lengths.append(1024)
    if time.monotonic() < deadline-900: lengths.append(2048)
    if time.monotonic() < deadline-250: lengths.append(512)
    for n in lengths:
        rem=deadline - time.monotonic()
        if rem < 120: break
        # cap time for this length
        if n==1024: length_time=min(rem*0.45, 700)
        elif n==2048: length_time=min(rem*0.5, 600)
        else: length_time=min(rem*0.3, 300)
        length_deadline=time.monotonic()+max(60, length_time)
        starts=generate_start_list(n, parent, rng, limit=30 if n<=1024 else 20)
        scored=[]
        for idx,(name,a) in enumerate(starts): ...
        if not scored: continue
        scored.sort()
        topk = scored[:min(5, len(scored))] if n<=1024 else scored[:min(3,len(scored))]
        # Optionally include current best.a resampled to n if not in starts
        if n != tracker.best.size:
           a_best_n=resample_seq(tracker.best,n)
           J=compute_J(a_best_n); insert? We can append candidate and sort.
        # We'll just topk from starts; maybe miss best resample. Add best resampled with unique idx after starts.
        # In generate, parent included if parent not None but current best may be from previous length not parent. Add explicitly.
        if tracker.best is not None:
           abn=resample_seq(tracker.best,n); J=compute_J(abn); if finite: scored.append((J, len(starts), 'best_resample', abn)); scored.sort()
        # take top 5 after
        topk=scored[:min(5 if n<=1024 else 3, len(scored))]
        for J0,idx,name,a in topk:
            if time.monotonic() >= length_deadline: break
            x0=make_x_from_a(a)
            optimize_x(x0,n,min(length_deadline, deadline-120),tracker,name,label_prefix=f'n{n}_{name}')
    # final LP
    if tracker.best is not None and time.monotonic() < deadline - 30:
        n=tracker.best.size
        use_full = n <= 1024
        top_frac = 1.0 if n <= 512 else 0.65
        time_limit = 2.0 if n <= 512 else (1.2 if n<=1024 else 0.8)
        lp_mirror(tracker.best, deadline, tracker, use_full, top_frac, time_limit, max_no_improve=250)
    # final L-BFGS if time
    if tracker.best is not None and time.monotonic() < deadline - 10:
        x=make_x_from_a(tracker.best)
        for tau, mf in [(1000, 1000), (5000, 800)]:
           if time.monotonic() >= deadline: break
           x_new=run_lbfgs(x, tau, mf, maxiter=500)
           a_new=softmax_from_x(x_new); J=compute_J(a_new)
           if J < tracker.best_J:
              x=x_new; tracker.update_score(a_new,J,'final_lbfgs')
    # final safety return
    if tracker.best is None: return [1.0]*128? but evaluate would 2; better fallback parent ones.
    return [float(v) for v in tracker.best]
```

Now, class `BestTracker` methods. We need avoid name conflict with built-in? fine.
```
class BestTracker:
    def __init__(self):
        self.best=None; self.best_J=float('inf')
    def update_score(self,a,J,label=''):
        if a is None: return
        a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64)
        if a.size==0: return
        a=np.clip(a,0.0,1000.0)
        s=a.sum()
        if s<=0 or not np.isfinite(J) or not np.isfinite(s): return
        a=a/s
        if J < self.best_J:
             self.best=a.copy(); self.best_J=float(J)
             print(f'  update J={J:.8f} n={a.size} {label}', flush=True)
    def update(self,a,label=''):
        if a is None: return
        a=np.asarray(a,float); a=np.clip(a,0,1000); s=a.sum(); if s<=0: return
        a=a/s; J=compute_J(a); self.update_score(a,J,label)
```
Normalizing in update and update_score; if caller already normalized, okay.

`compute_J` also normalizes; update computes J after normalization. Good.

In line search, after normalizing trial and J, call update_score.

`adam_optimize_x` code:
```
def adam_optimize_x(x0, tau, max_iters, lr, tracker, label):
    global OBJ_TAU
    OBJ_TAU=float(tau)
    x=np.asarray(x0,float).copy(); x-=x.mean()
    m=np.zeros_like(x); v=np.zeros_like(x)
    beta1=0.9; beta2=0.999; eps=1e-8
    for it in range(int(max_iters)):
        val,g=obj_grad(x)
        if not finite val or all zero grad: break
        it1=it+1
        m=beta1*m+(1-beta1)*g
        v=beta2*v+(1-beta2)*(g*g)
        mhat=m/(1-beta1**it1)
        vhat=v/(1-beta2**it1)
        x=x-lr*mhat/(np.sqrt(vhat)+eps)
        if not finite x: x=x0; break
        x-=x.mean()
        if it % 25 ==0:
             a=softmax_from_x(x); J=compute_J(a)
             tracker.update_score(a,J,label)
    return x
```
But `x0` fixed; if x becomes bad, resetting to x0 loses progress. Instead keep local best_x. We can return best_x, not x. Update local best when J<local_J. But `tracker.update_score` already updates global. For return, maintain local. Need local_J initialize. We'll do.
```
local_x=x; local_a=softmax; local_J=compute_J
for...
  if compute J < local_J: local_x=x.copy(); local_J=J
return local_x
```
This calls compute J at start and every 25. Good.

```
def optimize_x(x0, n, stop_time, tracker, label):
    local_x=x0.copy(); local_a=softmax_from_x(local_x); local_J=compute_J(local_a)
    tracker.update_score(local_a, local_J, label)
    # Adam if time
    if time.monotonic() < stop_time - 30:
        x_try = adam_optimize_x(local_x, 20.0, 800 if n<=1024 else 400, 0.05, tracker, label+'_adam')
        a_try=softmax_from_x(x_try); J_try=compute_J(a_try)
        if J_try < local_J:
             local_x=x_try; local_J=J_try
    stages = [(50, 800), (200, 1800), (1000, 2500)]
    for tau,mf in stages:
        if time.monotonic() >= stop_time - 20: break
        if n > 1500: mf = max(200, mf//3)
        elif n > 1000: mf = max(300, mf//2)
        else: mf = int(mf)
        x_try=run_lbfgs(local_x, tau, mf, maxiter=1000)
        a_try=softmax_from_x(x_try); J_try=compute_J(a_try)
        tracker.update_score(a_try, J_try, label+f'_tau{tau}')
        if J_try < local_J:
             local_x=x_try; local_J=J_try
    return local_x, local_J
```

`load_parent` top-level:
```
def load_parent():
    try:
        p = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64)
        if p.ndim != 1 or p.size == 0: return None
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(p)): return None
        p = np.maximum(p, 0.0)
        if p.sum() <= 0: return None
        return p
    except Exception:
        return None
```

`resample_seq`:
```
def resample_seq(src, dest_n):
    src=np.asarray(src,float)
    if src.size==0: return np.ones(dest_n)
    if src.size==dest_n: return src.copy()
    t_old=np.linspace(0,1,src.size)
    t_new=np.linspace(0,1,dest_n)
    out=np.interp(t_new,t_old,src)
    return np.maximum(out,0.0)
```

`generate_start_list`: Ensure all arrays length n; normalize not inside? We can not normalize; propose will. But some starts sum zero (sine clip all zero?) skip.
Implementation details:
```
def generate_start_list(n, parent, rng, limit=80):
    starts=[]
    t=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n,endpoint=False)
    if parent is not None:
       p=resample_seq(parent,n)
       if p.sum()>0: starts.append(('parent', p.copy()))
       p2=np.maximum(p + 0.05*float(p.mean())*rng.normal(0.0,1.0,n), 0.0)
       if p2.sum()>0: starts.append(('parent_noise', p2))
    idx=np.arange(1,n+1,dtype=float)
    # left root offsets
    for off in [0,1,5,10,20,int(0.02*n),int(0.05*n),int(0.1*n),int(0.2*n)]:
       if off <0 or off>=n-1: continue
       left=np.arange(off+1, off+n+1, dtype=float)**(-0.5)
       right=left[::-1]
       if left.sum()>0: starts.append((f'rootL{off}', left))
       starts.append((f'rootR{off}', right))
    # alpha
    for alpha in [0.3,0.4,0.5,0.6,0.75]:
       a=idx**(-alpha)
       starts.append((f'powL{alpha}', a))
       starts.append((f'powR{alpha}', a[::-1]))
    # beta skew
    eps=1e-3
    shapes=[(1,1.5),(1,2),(1,3),(2,1),(1.5,1),(3,1),(0.5,1),(1,0.5),(0.5,0.5),(2,0.5),(0.5,2)]
    for shp in shapes:
       a=(t+eps)**(shp[0]-1.0)*(1.0-t+eps)**(shp[1]-1.0)
       a=np.maximum(a,0.0)
       if a.sum()>0: starts.append((f'beta{shp[0]}_{shp[1]}', a))
    # polynomial one-sided
    for p0 in [0.5,1.0,1.5,2.0,3.0]:
       a=(1.0-t)**p0
       if a.sum()>0: starts.append((f'dec{p0}', a))
       a=t**p0
       starts.append((f'inc{p0}', a))
    # exponential
    for lam in [0.5,1.0,2.0,5.0]:
       a=np.exp(-lam*t); starts.append((f'expL{lam}', a))
       a=np.exp(-lam*(1.0-t)); starts.append((f'expR{lam}', a))
    # sine
    for amp in [0.3,0.6,0.9]:
       for freq in [1,2]:
          for phase in [0.0, np.pi/2.0, np.pi, 3.0*np.pi/2.0]:
             a=1.0+amp*np.sin(2.0*np.pi*float(freq)*t+phase)
             a=np.maximum(a,0.0)
             if a.sum()>0: starts.append((f'sin{amp}_{freq}_{int(phase*2)}', a))
    # random smooth
    for r in range(4):
       f=rng.integers(1,4,size=3)
       ph=rng.uniform(0.0,2.0*np.pi,size=3)
       am=rng.uniform(0.3,1.0,size=3)
       a=np.ones(n)
       for j in range(3):
          a += float(am[j])*np.cos(2.0*np.pi*float(f[j])*t+float(ph[j]))
       a=np.maximum(a,0.0)
       if a.sum()>0: starts.append((f'rand{r}', a))
    if len(starts)>limit:
       # keep deterministic mix? We can take first limit? To avoid losing root offsets due order, root first then many. Maybe random sample with seed.
       # Use rng to sample indices without lambda
       idxs=np.random.default_rng(int(rng.integers(0,2**31))).choice(len(starts), size=limit, replace=False) if len(starts)>limit else np.arange(len(starts))
       # But creating new rng okay.
       starts=[starts[i] for i in idxs]
    return starts
```
But `np.random.default_rng` inside function? We have rng; can use `idxs = rng.choice(len(starts), size=limit, replace=False)`. `rng.choice` returns array. Then list comprehension okay (not lambda). Good.

Now, let's write `lp_mirror` with `time_limit_lp` parameter.
```
def lp_mirror(a0, final_deadline, tracker, use_full, top_frac, time_limit, max_no_improve=200):
   a=...
   best_J_local=tracker.best_J
   no=0; it=0
   while time.monotonic() < final_deadline:
       it+=1
       c=fast_conv(a); M=float(c.max())
       if M<=0 or not finite: break
       g=solve_lp(a,M,c,top_frac,time_limit)
       if g is None or g.size==0:
           no += 1
       else:
           sg=g.sum()
           if sg > 1e-12:
               gn=g/sg
               current_J=best_J_local? use compute_J(a) maybe
               improved=False
               diff=float(np.max(np.abs(gn-a)))
               base=min(0.8, max(0.05, diff*2.0))
               vals=[base, base*0.5, base*0.25, 0.05,0.1,0.2,0.3,0.5,0.7]
               cand=[]
               for tt in vals:
                   if 0.001<tt<0.999:
                       dup=False
                       for cval in cand:
                           if abs(cval-tt)<1e-5: dup=True; break
                       if not dup: cand.append(tt)
               for t in cand:
                   trial=(1.0-t)*a + t*gn
                   s=trial.sum()
                   if s<=0: continue
                   trial=np.maximum(trial,0.0)/s
                   J=compute_J(trial)
                   if J < tracker.best_J - 1e-12:
                        tracker.update_score(trial,J,'lp')
                        a=trial.copy(); best_J_local=tracker.best_J; improved=True; break
                   if J < current_J - 1e-12:
                        a=trial.copy(); improved=True; break
               if improved: no=0
               else: no += 1
           else: no += 1
       if no > max_no_improve:
           # perturb
           rng=np.random.default_rng(1234567+it)
           scaled=no//max_no_improve
           amp=0.1/(1.0+0.3*scaled)
           tt_arr=np.linspace(0,1,a.size,endpoint=False)
           for freq in [1,2,3]:
               ph=rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi); ampf=rng.uniform(0.5,1.5)
               a += amp*ampf*np.cos(2*np.pi*freq*tt_arr+ph)
           a=np.maximum(a,1e-9); a/=a.sum(); no=0
       if it % 200 ==0: print...
       if time.monotonic() >= final_deadline: break
```

```
def compute_J(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.size==0: return inf
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(a)): return inf
    a = np.maximum(a,0.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 0.0: return inf
    a = a/s
    n = len(a)
    c = fast_conv(a)
    M = float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 0.0
    if not np.isfinite(M): return inf
    return float(2.0 * n * M)
```

```python
"""
Search for non-negative step-function heights minimizing
J(a) = 2*n*max(conv(a,a)) / (sum(a))^2.

Strategy:
- Normalize sequences to sum 1.
- Use diverse one-dimensional initializations (offset one-sided stable
  profiles, beta shapes, exponentials, sine perturbations, resampled parent).
- Optimize on the log-softmax manifold with L-BFGS using a smooth
  log-sum-exp approximation to the max of the self-convolution, with
  increasing temperature.
- A lightweight Adam stage on the same parameterization is used to warm up
  candidates.
- Polish with an LP-based mirror-descent method using the current sequence
  to form linear constraints and a best-response direction, with line search
  and periodic structured perturbations.
"""

import time
import numpy as np
from scipy.optimize import minimize, linprog
from scipy.sparse import csr_matrix, diags as sparse_diags


OBJ_TAU = 1.0


def fast_conv(a):
    """Linear self-convolution via FFT."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = len(a)
    if n == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    out_len = 2 * n - 1
    size = 1
    while size < out_len:
        size <<= 1
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, size)
    c = np.fft.irfft(fa * fa, size)[:out_len]
    c = np.where(c < 0.0, 0.0, c)
    return c


def fast_cross_conv(a, b):
    """Linear convolution of a and b via FFT."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    b = np.asarray(b, dtype=np.float64)
    na = len(a)
    nb = len(b)
    if na == 0 or nb == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    out_len = na + nb - 1
    size = 1
    while size < out_len:
        size <<= 1
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, size)
    fb = np.fft.rfft(b, size)
    c = np.fft.irfft(fa * fb, size)[:out_len]
    return c


def compute_J(a):
    """Scale-invariant objective for normalized non-negative a."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.size == 0:
        return float("inf")
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(a)):
        return float("inf")
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 0.0:
        return float("inf")
    a = a / s
    n = len(a)
    c = fast_conv(a)
    if c.size == 0:
        return float("inf")
    m = float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(m):
        return float("inf")
    return float(2.0 * n * m)


def softmax_from_x(x):
    """Stable softmax, centered for numerical stability."""
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64)
    if x.size == 0:
        return x.copy()
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(x)):
        return np.full_like(x, 1.0 / x.size, dtype=np.float64)
    x = x - x.mean()
    x = x - x.max()
    ex = np.exp(x)
    s = float(ex.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        ex = np.ones_like(x, dtype=np.float64)
        s = float(x.size)
    return ex / s


def make_x_from_a(a):
    """Initialize log-softmax parameters from a positive vector."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.size == 0:
        return a.copy()
    a = np.maximum(a, np.finfo(np.float64).tiny)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 0.0:
        return np.zeros_like(a, dtype=np.float64)
    a = a / s
    x = np.log(a)
    x = x - x.mean()
    return x


def obj_grad(x):
    """
    Objective and gradient for a smooth log-sum-exp approximation of
    log(max conv(a,a)) with a = softmax(x).
    """
    global OBJ_TAU

    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64)
    n = len(x)
    if n == 0:
        return 0.0, np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(x)):
        return float("inf"), np.zeros_like(x, dtype=np.float64)

    a = softmax_from_x(x)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 0.0:
        return float("inf"), np.zeros_like(x, dtype=np.float64)
    a = a / s

    c = fast_conv(a)
    if c.size == 0:
        return float("inf"), np.zeros_like(x, dtype=np.float64)
    c = np.where(c < 0.0, 0.0, c)
    s2 = s * s
    d = c / s2

    tau = float(OBJ_TAU)
    if tau <= 0.0:
        tau = 1.0

    shift = float(np.max(d))
    z = tau * (d - shift)
    expz = np.exp(z)
    ez = float(expz.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(ez) or ez <= 0.0:
        p = np.full_like(d, 1.0 / d.size, dtype=np.float64)
        val_h = shift
    else:
        p = expz / ez
        val_h = shift + float(np.log(ez)) / tau

    # Gradient of sum_k p_k c_k with respect to a.
    tmp = fast_cross_conv(p, a[::-1])
    if tmp.size >= 2 * n - 1:
        g_a = 2.0 * tmp[n - 1:2 * n - 1]
    else:
        g_a = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    # Chain rule through a = softmax(x).
    shift_grad = float(np.dot(a, g_a))
    g_x = a * (g_a - shift_grad)

    scale = float(n)
    val = float((val_h + np.log(float(n))) * scale + 1.0)
    g_x = g_x * scale

    if not np.all(np.isfinite(g_x)):
        g_x = np.zeros_like(x, dtype=np.float64)
    if not np.isfinite(val):
        val = 1e300
    return float(val), g_x


def run_lbfgs(x0, tau, maxfun, maxiter):
    """Run L-BFGS-B for the smooth objective."""
    global OBJ_TAU

    if x0 is None or len(x0) == 0:
        return x0
    x = np.asarray(x0, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(x)):
        x = np.zeros_like(x, dtype=np.float64)
    x = x - x.mean()

    OBJ_TAU = float(tau)
    try:
        res = minimize(
            obj_grad,
            x,
            method="L-BFGS-B",
            jac=True,
            options={
                "maxiter": int(maxiter),
                "maxfun": int(maxfun),
                "ftol": 1e-10,
                "gtol": 1e-7,
                "maxls": 20,
                "disp": False,
            },
        )
        if res.x is not None and np.all(np.isfinite(res.x)):
            return res.x - res.x.mean()
    except Exception as e:
        print("      L-BFGS error:", repr(e), flush=True)
    return x


def adam_optimize_x(x0, tau, max_iters, lr, tracker, label):
    """Short Adam warm-up in log-softmax coordinates."""
    global OBJ_TAU

    if x0 is None or len(x0) == 0:
        return x0
    x = np.asarray(x0, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(x)):
        x = np.zeros_like(x, dtype=np.float64)
    x = x - x.mean()

    local_x = x.copy()
    local_a = softmax_from_x(local_x)
    local_j = compute_J(local_a)
    if tracker is not None:
        tracker.update_score(local_a, local_j, label)

    beta1 = 0.9
    beta2 = 0.999
    eps = 1e-8
    m = np.zeros_like(x, dtype=np.float64)
    v = np.zeros_like(x, dtype=np.float64)

    OBJ_TAU = float(tau)
    ti = 0
    for it in range(int(max_iters)):
        ti += 1
        val, g = obj_grad(x)
        if not np.isfinite(val):
            break
        if not np.any(np.abs(g) > 0.0):
            break

        m = beta1 * m + (1.0 - beta1) * g
        v = beta2 * v + (1.0 - beta2) * (g * g)
        mhat = m / (1.0 - beta1 ** ti)
        vhat = v / (1.0 - beta2 ** ti)

        x = x - float(lr) * mhat / (np.sqrt(vhat) + eps)
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(x)):
            break
        x = x - x.mean()

        if it % 25 == 24:
            a = softmax_from_x(x)
            j = compute_J(a)
            if np.isfinite(j) and j < local_j:
                local_j = j
                local_x = x.copy()
                if tracker is not None:
                    tracker.update_score(a, j, label)

    return local_x


def resample_seq(src, dest_n):
    """Resample a 1D non-negative sequence to dest_n by linear interpolation."""
    src = np.asarray(src, dtype=np.float64)
    if src.size == 0:
        return np.ones(dest_n, dtype=np.float64)
    if src.size == dest_n:
        return src.copy()
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(src)):
        return np.ones(dest_n, dtype=np.float64)
    t_old = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, src.size)
    t_new = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, dest_n)
    out = np.interp(t_new, t_old, src)
    return np.maximum(out, 0.0)


def generate_start_list(n, parent, rng, limit=80):
    """Generate diverse one-sided profiles for length n."""
    starts = []
    t = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, endpoint=False)

    if parent is not None:
        p = resample_seq(parent, n)
        if p.sum() > 0.0:
            starts.append(("parent", p.copy()))
        base = max(float(p.mean()), 1e-12)
        noisy = p + 0.05 * base * rng.normal(0.0, 1.0, size=n)
        noisy = np.maximum(noisy, 0.0)
        if noisy.sum() > 0.0:
            starts.append(("parent_noise", noisy))

    idx = np.arange(1, n + 1, dtype=np.float64)

    # Offset one-sided stable profiles.
    offs = [0, 1, 5, 10, 20, int(0.02 * n), int(0.05 * n), int(0.10 * n), int(0.20 * n)]
    for off in offs:
        if off < 0 or off >= max(1, n - 1):
            continue
        left = np.arange(off + 1, off + n + 1, dtype=np.float64) ** (-0.5)
        right = left[::-1]
        if left.sum() > 0.0:
            starts.append((f"rootL{off}", left))
        if right.sum() > 0.0:
            starts.append((f"rootR{off}", right))

    # Power laws.
    for alpha in [0.3, 0.4, 0.5, 0.6, 0.75]:
        a = idx ** (-alpha)
        starts.append((f"powL{alpha}", a))
        starts.append((f"powR{alpha}", a[::-1]))

    # Skew beta-like profiles.
    eps = 1e-3
    shapes = [
        (1.0, 1.5), (1.0, 2.0), (1.0, 3.0),
        (2.0, 1.0), (1.5, 1.0), (3.0, 1.0),
        (0.5, 1.0), (1.0, 0.5), (0.5, 0.5),
        (2.0, 0.5), (0.5, 2.0)
    ]
    for shp in shapes:
        a = (t + eps) ** (shp[0] - 1.0) * (1.0 - t + eps) ** (shp[1] - 1.0)
        a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
        if a.sum() > 0.0:
            starts.append((f"beta{shp[0]}_{shp[1]}", a))

    # One-sided polynomials.
    for p0 in [0.5, 1.0, 1.5, 2.0, 3.0]:
        a = (1.0 - t) ** p0
        if a.sum() > 0.0:
            starts.append((f"dec{p0}", a))
        a2 = t ** p0
        if a2.sum() > 0.0:
            starts.append((f"inc{p0}", a2))

    # Exponentials.
    for lam in [0.5, 1.0, 2.0, 5.0]:
        a = np.exp(-lam * t)
        starts.append((f"expL{lam}", a))
        a = np.exp(-lam * (1.0 - t))
        starts.append((f"expR{lam}", a))

    # Bounded sine perturbations.
    for amp in [0.3, 0.6, 0.9]:
        for freq in [1, 2]:
            for phase in [0.0, np.pi / 2.0, np.pi, 3.0 * np.pi / 2.0]:
                a = 1.0 + amp * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * float(freq) * t + phase)
                a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
                if a.sum() > 0.0:
                    starts.append((f"sin{amp}_{freq}_{int(phase * 2.0)}", a))

    # Smooth random wave blends.
    for r in range(4):
        f = rng.integers(1, 4, size=3)
        ph = rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi, size=3)
        am = rng.uniform(0.3, 1.0, size=3)
        a = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
        for j in range(3):
            a += float(am[j]) * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * float(f[j]) * t + float(ph[j]))
        a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
        if a.sum() > 0.0:
            starts.append((f"rand{r}", a))

    if len(starts) > limit:
        starts = starts[:limit]
    return starts


def build_conv_matrix(a):
    """
    Build sparse matrix A with (A*g) = conv(a,g).
    Shape: (2n-1, n).
    """
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = len(a)
    m = 2 * n - 1
    diag_arrays = []
    offsets = []
    for d in range(n):
        diag_arrays.append(np.full(n, float(a[d]), dtype=np.float64))
        offsets.append(d)
    A = sparse_diags(diag_arrays, offsets=offsets, shape=(m, n)).tocsr()
    A.eliminate_zeros()
    return A


def solve_lp(a, M, c, top_frac, time_limit):
    """
    LP direction:
      max sum(g)
      s.t. conv(a,g) <= M, g >= 0.
    Uses active constraints and a conservative upper bound.
    """
    n = len(a)
    if M <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(M):
        return None

    L = 2 * n - 1
    top_frac = float(top_frac)
    if top_frac >= 1.0:
        active = np.arange(L, dtype=int)
    else:
        k = max(20, int(L * top_frac))
        if k >= L:
            active = np.arange(L, dtype=int)
        else:
            active = np.argpartition(c, -k)[-k:]

    A = build_conv_matrix(a)
    if active.size < L:
        A_sub = A[active, :]
    else:
        A_sub = A.tocsr()

    invM = 1.0 / M
    A_use = A_sub * invM

    ub = max(20.0 / float(n), 2.0 * float(np.max(a)), 1e-4)
    ub = min(ub, 0.5)

    c_obj = -np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    b_ub = np.ones(active.size, dtype=np.float64)

    def check_g(g):
        if g is None:
            return None
        g = np.asarray(g, dtype=np.float64)
        if g.size != n or not np.all(np.isfinite(g)):
            return None
        g = np.maximum(g, 0.0)
        ag = fast_cross_conv(a, g)
        if ag.size == 0:
            return None
        viol = float(np.max(ag)) - M
        if viol <= 1e-9 + 1e-4 * M:
            return g
        return g if viol <= 0.05 * M else None

    try:
        res = linprog(
            c_obj,
            A_ub=A_use,
            b_ub=b_ub,
            bounds=(0.0, ub),
            method="highs",
            options={
                "time_limit": float(time_limit),
                "presolve": True,
            },
        )
        if res.success and res.x is not None:
            g = check_g(res.x)
            if g is not None:
                return g
        elif res.x is not None:
            g = check_g(res.x)
            if g is not None:
                return g
    except Exception:
        pass

    # Fallback to all constraints when modest size.
    if active.size < L and n <= 1200:
        try:
            A_full = A * invM
            res2 = linprog(
                c_obj,
                A_ub=A_full,
                b_ub=np.ones(L, dtype=np.float64),
                bounds=(0.0, ub),
                method="highs",
                options={
                    "time_limit": float(time_limit),
                    "presolve": True,
                },
            )
            if res2.success and res2.x is not None:
                return check_g(res2.x)
        except Exception:
            pass

    return None


def lp_mirror(a0, final_deadline, tracker, use_full, top_frac, time_limit, max_no_improve=250):
    """LP mirror-descent polish on the exact max objective."""
    a = np.asarray(a0, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.size == 0:
        return
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 0.0:
        return
    a = a / s

    current_J = compute_J(a)
    if tracker is not None and np.isfinite(current_J):
        tracker.update_score(a, current_J, "lp_start")

    no_improve = 0
    it = 0

    while time.monotonic() < final_deadline:
        it += 1

        c = fast_conv(a)
        M = float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 0.0
        if M <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(M):
            break

        g = solve_lp(a, M, c, 1.0 if use_full else top_frac, time_limit)
        improved = False

        if g is not None and g.size == a.size:
            sg = float(g.sum())
            if sg > 1e-12:
                gn = g / sg
                diff = float(np.max(np.abs(gn - a)))
                base_t = min(0.8, max(0.05, 2.0 * diff))
                vals = [
                    base_t, base_t * 0.5, base_t * 0.25,
                    0.05, 0.10, 0.20, 0.30, 0.50, 0.70
                ]
                cand = []
                for tt in vals:
                    if 0.002 < tt < 0.998:
                        dup = False
                        for cv in cand:
                            if abs(cv - tt) < 1e-5:
                                dup = True
                                break
                        if not dup:
                            cand.append(float(tt))

                for tt in cand:
                    trial = (1.0 - tt) * a + tt * gn
                    trial = np.maximum(trial, 0.0)
                    ts = float(trial.sum())
                    if ts <= 0.0:
                        continue
                    trial = trial / ts
                    J = compute_J(trial)
                    if not np.isfinite(J):
                        continue
                    if tracker is not None and J < tracker.best_J - 1e-12:
                        tracker.update_score(trial, J, f"lp_it{it}")
                        a = trial.copy()
                        current_J = J
                        improved = True
                        break
                    if J < current_J - 1e-12:
                        a = trial.copy()
                        current_J = J
                        improved = True
                        break

        if improved:
            no_improve = 0
        else:
            no_improve += 1

        if no_improve > max_no_improve:
            rng = np.random.default_rng(1234567 + it)
            level = no_improve // max_no_improve
            amp = 0.10 / (1.0 + 0.35 * level)
            tt_arr = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, a.size, endpoint=False)
            pert = np.zeros_like(a, dtype=np.float64)
            for freq in [1, 2, 3]:
                ph = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
                af = float(rng.uniform(0.5, 1.5))
                pert += af * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * freq * tt_arr + ph)
            a = a + amp * pert
            a = np.maximum(a, 1e-9)
            as_ = float(a.sum())
            if as_ > 0.0:
                a = a / as_
            no_improve = 0

        if it % 200 == 0:
            print(
                f"      lp mirror: it={it} J={current_J:.8f} no_improve={no_improve}",
                flush=True
            )

        if time.monotonic() >= final_deadline:
            break


def optimize_x(x0, n, stop_time, tracker, label):
    """Adam warm-up followed by multi-temperature L-BFGS."""
    if x0 is None or len(x0) == 0:
        return

    local_x = np.asarray(x0, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    local_a = softmax_from_x(local_x)
    local_j = compute_J(local_a)
    if tracker is not None and np.isfinite(local_j):
        tracker.update_score(local_a, local_j, label)

    if time.monotonic() < stop_time - 30:
        adam_iters = 600 if n <= 1200 else 300
        x_try = adam_optimize_x(local_x, 20.0, adam_iters, 0.10, tracker, label + "_adam")
        a_try = softmax_from_x(x_try)
        J_try = compute_J(a_try)
        if np.isfinite(J_try) and J_try < local_j:
            local_j = J_try
            local_x = x_try

    if n <= 768:
        stages = [
            (20.0, 100, 300),
            (100.0, 150, 800),
            (500.0, 200, 1500),
        ]
        extra = (2000.0, 250, 2000)
    elif n <= 1200:
        stages = [
            (20.0, 100, 250),
            (100.0, 150, 700),
            (500.0, 200, 1200),
        ]
        extra = (2000.0, 200, 1500)
    else:
        stages = [
            (20.0, 80, 200),
            (100.0, 120, 500),
            (500.0, 150, 800),
        ]
        extra = (1000.0, 150, 1000)

    for tau, maxit, mfun in stages:
        if time.monotonic() >= stop_time - 20:
            break
        time_left = stop_time - time.monotonic()
        mf = int(min(mfun, max(100, int(time_left * 50))))
        x_try = run_lbfgs(local_x, tau, mf, maxit)
        a_try = softmax_from_x(x_try)
        J_try = compute_J(a_try)
        if tracker is not None and np.isfinite(J_try):
            tracker.update_score(a_try, J_try, label + f"_tau{int(tau)}")
        if np.isfinite(J_try) and J_try < local_j:
            local_j = J_try
            local_x = x_try

    if time.monotonic() < stop_time - 30:
        tau, maxit, mfun = extra
        time_left = stop_time - time.monotonic()
        mf = int(min(mfun, max(100, int(time_left * 50))))
        x_try = run_lbfgs(local_x, tau, mf, maxit)
        a_try = softmax_from_x(x_try)
        J_try = compute_J(a_try)
        if tracker is not None and np.isfinite(J_try):
            tracker.update_score(a_try, J_try, label + f"_extra{int(tau)}")
        if np.isfinite(J_try) and J_try < local_j:
            local_j = J_try
            local_x = x_try

    return local_x


class BestTracker:
    def __init__(self):
        self.best = None
        self.best_J = float("inf")

    def update_score(self, a, J, label=""):
        if a is None:
            return
        a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
        if a.size == 0:
            return
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(a)):
            return
        a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
        s = float(a.sum())
        if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(J):
            return
        a = a / s
        J = float(J)
        if J < self.best_J:
            self.best = a.copy()
            self.best_J = J
            if label:
                print(f"  ** J={J:.8f} n={a.size} [{label}]", flush=True)
            else:
                print(f"  ** J={J:.8f} n={a.size}", flush=True)

    def update(self, a, label=""):
        if a is None:
            return
        a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
        if a.size == 0:
            return
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(a)):
            return
        a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
        s = float(a.sum())
        if s <= 0.0:
            return
        a = a / s
        J = compute_J(a)
        self.update_score(a, J, label)


def load_parent():
    """Load height_sequence_1 if present."""
    try:
        p = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64)
        if p.ndim != 1 or p.size == 0:
            return None
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(p)):
            return None
        p = np.maximum(p, 0.0)
        if float(p.sum()) <= 0.0:
            return None
        return p
    except Exception:
        return None


def process_length(n, parent, rng, length_deadline, global_deadline, tracker):
    """Generate, screen, and optimize candidates of a fixed length."""
    starts = generate_start_list(n, parent, rng, limit=80)
    scored = []

    for idx, (name, a) in enumerate(starts):
        a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
        if a.size != n or not np.all(np.isfinite(a)):
            continue
        a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
        s = float(a.sum())
        if s <= 0.0:
            continue
        a = a / s
        J = compute_J(a)
        if np.isfinite(J):
            scored.append((J, idx, name, a))

    if tracker.best is not None:
        abn = resample_seq(tracker.best, n)
        Jbn = compute_J(abn)
        if np.isfinite(Jbn):
            scored.append((Jbn, len(starts), "best_resample", abn))

    if not scored:
        return

    scored.sort()
    top_count = 5 if n <= 1024 else 3
    top = scored[:top_count]

    for J0, idx, name, a in top:
        if time.monotonic() >= length_deadline:
            break
        stop = min(length_deadline, global_deadline - 120.0)
        if time.monotonic() >= stop:
            break
        x0 = make_x_from_a(a)
        optimize_x(x0, n, stop, tracker, f"n{n}_{name}")


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1500, **kwargs):
    """Main entry point: search and return the best sequence found."""
    rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed))
    start_time = time.monotonic()
    deadline = start_time + float(budget_s) - 30.0
    tracker = BestTracker()

    parent = load_parent()
    if parent is not None:
        tracker.update(parent, "parent")
        print(f"Parent loaded: n={parent.size} J={tracker.best_J:.8f}", flush=True)
    else:
        parent = np.ones(256, dtype=np.float64)
        tracker.update(parent, "fallback_ones")

    # Choose lengths to explore. Try 1024 first, then longer if time permits,
    # then a fast 512 polish.
    lengths = []
    if float(budget_s) > 500:
        lengths.append(1024)
    if float(budget_s) > 1000:
        lengths.append(2048)
    if float(budget_s) > 400:
        lengths.append(512)

    for n in lengths:
        rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
        if rem < 150.0:
            break

        if n == 2048 and rem < 850.0:
            continue
        if n == 1024 and rem < 450.0:
            continue
        if n == 512 and rem < 250.0:
            continue

        if n == 2048:
            lt = min(rem * 0.5, 600.0)
        elif n == 1024:
            lt = min(rem * 0.5, 700.0)
        else:
            lt = min(rem * 0.4, 300.0)

        length_deadline = time.monotonic() + max(60.0, lt)
        print(f"\n=== length {n}, budget ~{length_deadline - time.monotonic():.0f}s ===", flush=True)
        process_length(n, parent, rng, length_deadline, deadline, tracker)

    # Final LP polish.
    if tracker.best is not None and time.monotonic() < deadline - 30.0:
        nbest = tracker.best.size
        print(f"\n=== final LP polish, n={nbest}, time_left={deadline - time.monotonic():.0f}s ===", flush=True)
        use_full = nbest <= 1024
        if nbest <= 512:
            top_frac = 1.0
            time_limit = 2.0
        elif nbest <= 1024:
            top_frac = 0.65
            time_limit = 1.2
        else:
            top_frac = 0.40
            time_limit = 0.8
        lp_mirror(
            tracker.best,
            deadline,
            tracker,
            use_full,
            top_frac,
            time_limit,
            max_no_improve=250,
        )

    # Final high-temperature smooth polish, only if useful.
    if tracker.best is not None and time.monotonic() < deadline - 15.0:
        x = make_x_from_a(tracker.best)
        x, J_final = None, float("inf")
        for tau, mf in [(1000.0, 1200), (5000.0, 900)]:
            if time.monotonic() >= deadline:
                break
            x_try = run_lbfgs(x, tau, int(min(mf, max(100, int((deadline - time.monotonic()) * 50)))), 400)
            a_try = softmax_from_x(x_try)
            J_try = compute_J(a_try)
            if np.isfinite(J_try):
                tracker.update_score(a_try, J_try, f"final_smooth_{int(tau)}")
                if J_try < J_final:
                    x = x_try
                    J_final = J_try

    if tracker.best is None:
        return [1.0] * 256

    ans = np.asarray(tracker.best, dtype=np.float64)
    print(f"\nFINAL selected: J={tracker.best_J:.8f} n={ans.size}", flush=True)
    return [float(x) for x in ans]
```
